#2494. Ch-ch-ch-ch Changes !

On a beau porter le nom d’une mythique relique, on a beau fièrement afficher des valeurs en marge de la toile putacliquesque, on n’a rien contre un brin de toilette. Il faut vivre avec son temps dit l’adage. Alors poteaux-carrés évolue (sous vos yeux qu’on espère ébahis).

Né en 2005, époque où les Verts de Juju, Hellebuyck, Cartoche, Compan et Janot retrouvaient la D1 tandis que leurs supporters se déchiraient entre pro Anto et pro Bozzo, poteaux-carrés se voulait alors résolument passionné, indépendant, frondeur et rieur.

Le Furan a sinué dans les entrailles de Sainté, ce qui sera toujours plus glamour qu'une pâle eau coulant sous d'anonymes ponts, p² a vécu, grandi, jusqu'à fêter très discrètement ses 20 ans. Pourtant, fin 2024 avait germé dans les cerveaux vieillissants de ses fondateurs l’idée de célébrer en grande pompe ce bel âge à l’occasion du retour en Ligue 1 de la bande à ODO. Le timing semblait idéal, le contexte constituant un joli clin d’œil aux débuts de notre aventure. Lors d’une réunion débridée et fertile, les idées les plus folles pour marquer joliment le coup jaillirent en feu d’artifices.

Las. En fait de fête, l’ASSE nous offrit une saison en forme de chemin de croix, douchant nos enthousiasmes, mouillant nos pétards, freinant nos résolutions, justifiant notre procrastination.

Témoignant de son amitié en nous invitant à combattre notre détresse, de là-haut, Thérèse nous glissa alors que notre site n’avait pas un physique facile et qu’il y a temps pour prendre ses aises, et un temps pour prendre sur soi. Vexés, mais consciencieux, nous finîmes par nous y coller. Peut-être parce que les renoncements de la veille sont les aigreurs du lendemain, sûrement parce qu’on se le devait, à nous, mais surtout à vous, la communauté potonaute, les fidèles compagnons de cette belle aventure.

Alors Veryvel a enfilé son tablier, pris ses plus beaux pinceaux, cherché en vain son vinyle de Bowie avant - faute de mieux - de glisser Zouk Machine dans son mange disque et a nettoyé, balayé et astiqué du sol au plafond, kaz la toujou penpan a-t-il cru bon d’ajouter.

Oui pimpant, clinquant, affriolant, fringant ! Voilà l'ambitieux programme pour la vitrine (le site) comme pour le cœur du réacteur (le forum).

Revoir la forme pour mieux servir le fond, telle est l’intention.

Mais rassurez-vous s’il s’offre un lifting pour ses 20 piges, poteaux-carrés - revisitant le concept malmené du en même temps - change et reste le même. Dit autrement, comme le beau – oui – David, il se réinvente mais se reste fidèle : même ripoliné jusque dans les coins, poteaux-carrés est toujours préparé et emballé par des passionnés pour des passionnés. Certains s’en souviennent peut-être, il y a 21 ans, nous écrivions pour nous présenter que nous avions tout à la fois :

- le souci de vivre notre passion des Verts au présent tout en laissant au passé la place d’honneur qu’il mérite,

- le vœu de vivre notre passion dans la joie, la bonne humeur, la gaudriole,

- la folle prétention d’être décalé et drôle,

- la volonté d’être le plus nombreux possible pour prolonger les (d)ébats jusqu’au bout de la nuit, sur le forum ou sur le zinc, clavier au bout des doigts ou binouze à la main.

Si l'enveloppe s'est flétrie et méritait son lifting, notre vocation n'a pas pris une ride. Parce que, comme à l'époque, comme toujours, nous continuons de rêver à ce jour où le ballon caressera le poteau dans le bon sens, et rebondira du bon côté de la ligne, verte, forcément.

Réagir sur le forumLe débat continue entre potonautes.

Ce soir là, pour goûter à la légende, il fallait être à Geoffroy-Guichard. Et il ne fallait pas arriver en retard...

#2493. Feyenoord second best to Betis as Isco is on the spot for CL win

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

Feyenoord endured a frustrating evening in Spain as a penalty by Real Betis’s outstanding player, Isco, secured a 1-0 win for the La Liga side.

The Rotterdammers were second best for most of the game, but held out until the 59th minute, when Abde Ezzalzouli’s cross hit the arm of Jeremiah St. Juste in front of goal.

Isco, who was at the heart of Betis’s attacks all evening, grabbed the ball and gave Tjark Ernst no chance from the penalty spot.

Ernst had made good saves from former AZ Alkmaar striker Troy Parrott and Marc Batra to keep Feyenoord in the game in the first half, but Feyenoord had the best chance of the opening period when Gjivai Zechiël shot over the bar from close range.

The visitors had a claim for a penalty of their own turned down in the 68th minute when Givairo Read claimed he had been pushed by Ezzalzouli, but referee Simone Sozza waved the appeals away.

The result leaves Giovanni van Bronckhorst’s side in 26th place in the Champions League table, with Internazionale up next in De Kuip on November 3.

#2488. Lazio, accordo Mizuno in scadenza: Napoli a 18 milioni più bonus

Il contratto tecnico biancoceleste si avvicina alla scadenza; quello del Como terminerà il 30 giugno 2027.

Il contratto della Lazio con Mizuno è in scadenza: per il club biancoceleste si aprirà la possibilità di chiedere una sponsorizzazione tecnica più generosa di quella attuale. Secondo quanto riportato da TuttoMercatoWeb, Il confronto passa anche dalle cifre del Napoli, che con Armani è arrivato a 18 milioni di euro più 2,5 di bonus.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

L’intesa biancoceleste si avvicina alla conclusione in una fase in cui gli accordi tecnici dei club presentano valori molto diversi. Alla scadenza, la Lazio potrà puntare a condizioni economiche migliori rispetto a quelle previste dal contratto attuale.

Tra gli accordi richiamati nel confronto figurano anche quelli della Roma con Adidas e della Fiorentina con Joma. Le cifre dei due club offrono altri riferimenti accanto al contratto della Lazio.

Il Napoli ha rinnovato l’intesa con Armani dopo le prime stagioni da 9 milioni di euro più uno di bonus. Il nuovo accordo vale 18 milioni di parte fissa e altri 2,5 di bonus. Alla scadenza, prevista nel 2028 per l’intesa del 2023, è ritenuta probabile una revisione al rialzo, anche se resta da capire di quale entità. Aurelio De Laurentiis viene indicato come abile nell’ottenere condizioni favorevoli nei contratti commerciali.

Il quadro delle sponsorizzazioni entra in una nuova fase anche con l’accordo del Milan con Adidas: 500 milioni di euro in dieci anni a partire dal 2028. La Roma riceve invece 5 milioni più bonus dall’intesa con lo stesso marchio, una cifra giudicata bassa rispetto alle valutazioni degli altri club citati.

Per la Fiorentina, il contratto con Joma prevede complessivamente 20 milioni in cinque anni, pari a 4 milioni a stagione.

#2487. Contribute to help victims of the Pedrógão Grande tragedy

Forest fires occur every summer in Portugal but the tragedy that struck the community of Pedrógão Grande in the centre of the country is unprecedented. Over 60 people lost their lives, many trapped in their cars when trying to flee from the flames.

This Just Giving page is a simple way for people, wherever they are in the world, to make a contribution to help rebuild these communities and bring some comfort to those who lost everything in the blink of an eye. The money raised will be sent to a network of local authorities and charities working in the area affected. Thank you for your support.

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

Feyenoord preserved their four-point advantage over champions PSV after coming away with a point from Eindhoven thanks to an improved second-half performance.

PSV dominated the first half as they deployed a close-passing, tiki-taka style to stifle Feyenoord, but only had Guus Til’s well-taken volley after 20 minutes to show for it.

Ten minutes after the break Feyenoord equalised when Anis Hadj Moussa was given too much space to charge down the right flank and bend a shot inside the far post.

The Algerian had a good chance for a second towards the end, while PSV goalkeeper Matej Kovar had to be alert to keep out a header by Nacho Ferri from close range.

“We were by far the better team today and had the better chances,” said PSV coach Peter Bosz. “We started the second half badly and made a mistake, after which Hadj Moussa scored. And even then we could have won it.”

Ajax overtook PSV to close the gap at the top to three points with a hard-fought win at Sparta. Veteran striker Jens Toornstra scored for Sparta on his 500th Eredivisie appearance, but Abdellah Ouazane and Tolu Arokodare, the scorers against Atalanta in midweek, were on target again to claim the win for Ajax.

Twente moved up to fourth place as two late goals by Sondre Ørjasæter and Lucas Vennegoor of Hesselink earned a 2-0 win at AZ Alkmaar, who like Twente and PSV have 20 points.

Fortuna Sittard also snatched the points late on against Excelsior as Rodrigo Guth headed in a corner in injury time to keep the Limburg club in sixth place, two points further back.

At the other end of the table, the return of Adrie Koster as veteran coach brought no relief to Willem II, who crashed to a 6-1 home defeat to Cambuur.

The Tilburgers remain four points behind ADO Den Haag, who were unable to keep up the momentum of last week’s win against PSV as they lost 3-2 at home to Heerenveen.

Telstar lost by the same margin at Go Ahead Eagles, whose striker Søren Tengstedt netted twice in the first half to lead the Eredivisie scoring charts with eight goals.

#2485. Lazio, Hernanes crede nella tenuta stagionale e avverte l’Inter

Per l’ex centrocampista, i biancocelesti giocano con intensità; i nerazzurri devono ritrovare energia e fare i conti con alcune assenze.

Hernanes ritiene che la Lazio possa mantenere il proprio rendimento per tutto il campionato. L’ex centrocampista brasiliano ne ha parlato a margine di un evento del Sigma World di Roma, commentando la prima parte di stagione biancoceleste. Secondo quanto riportato da Agipronews, Ha poi rivolto la sua attenzione all’Inter, che considera ancora la squadra più forte ma dalla quale si aspetta una reazione.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

«La Lazio ha il potenziale per poter durare tutto il campionato», ha dichiarato Hernanes, secondo quanto riportato da Agipronews. Per il «Profeta», la squadra può contare su un allenatore vincente, che conosce la strada per la gloria e le ha trasmesso la propria onestà calcistica: scendere in campo dando tutto.

È questo, a suo giudizio, ciò che emerge dal gioco dei biancocelesti. Hernanes descrive una formazione intensa, con i valori giusti in ogni posizione, e sottolinea la qualità dei giocatori. «È una bellissima squadra che sta rendendo al 100%», ha aggiunto.

Parlando dell’altra sua ex squadra, Hernanes ha ricordato che nel calcio nessuna vittoria è acquisita prima di giocare: bisogna scendere in campo e dare il massimo. Pur ritenendo l’Inter la più forte, vede nei nerazzurri gli effetti della vittoria dello scorso anno. «Stanno ancora giocando con la pancia piena dopo la vittoria dello scorso anno», ha osservato.

Secondo il brasiliano, il calo di energia può avvenire inconsciamente. Finora, a suo avviso, l’energia impiegata era sufficiente; ora però l’Inter comincia a complicarsi la vita. Per questo, ha concluso il suo richiamo, i nerazzurri «devono svegliarsi».

#2483. Burkardt saves the day for Frankfurt in Paderborn as penalty king Atubolu delivers in different way

Adi Hütter surprisingly left Germany international Younes Ebnoutalib on the bench, but fellow striker Jonathan Burkardt gave them an early lead at the midpoint of a lively first-half. Ritsu Doan and Lillian Brassier had the game's first chances inside the first ten minutes.

Noah Atubolu's first save arrived minutes later as he parried away Gabriel Vidovic's curling effort. Against the run of play, the visitors took the lead as Fares Chaïbi punished Paderborn's disorganized defense with a brilliant assist for Burkardt.

Stefano Marino hit the crossbar five minutes later before Chaïbi nearly added a goal to his assist. The hosts got a deserved equalizer just before the half-time break as the unmarked Raphael Obermair nutmegged Atubolu with a quickfire finish.

After his assist to Obermair's goal, Ruben Müller nearly scored early after the restart before another decent chance for US midfielder Santiago Castaneda. Chaïbi also had a chance for his second direct goal involvement inside the lively ten minutes right after the half-time break.

With 25 minutes left to play, both coaches already made seven substitutions in total. 16-year-old midfielder Niko Ilicevic entered the scene for his second Bundesliga appearance, but Paderborn took the lead for the first time after he was dispossessed in the danger zone.

Brassier had a difficult game, and he committed a foul on substitute Marvin Pieringer inside the box. Atubolu, who saved all the previous six penalty-kicks he has faced at Freiburg and Frankfurt, dived the right way, but he wasn't able to deny Pieringer his first Paderborn goal.

Frankfurt's hopes of getting back to the game faced a huge blow a couple of minutes later as Elias Baum saw his second yellow card. Ralf Kettemann subbed off half-time substitute Deniz Zeitler in fear of a similar outcome, but the hosts failed to hold on to their slender lead.

Atubolu's penalty-kick record came to an end, but he guided Chaïbi's corner-kick to Burkardt's direction as the striker leveled the score with an unorthodox finish in the last action of the game.

Paderborn tied a club-record four Bundesliga games unbeaten run, but they have only beaten HSV in this run. Frankfurt are in eighth place as the Bundesliga will now take a second international break.

#2481. Vignes espère grappiller au moins un point

Entraîneur de la plus mauvaise équipe à l'extérieur de Ligue 2 (1 points sur 15), le Lavallois David Vignes (53 ans) s'est exprimé en conférence de presse avant d'affronter demain soir dans le Chaudron des leaders stéphanois intraitables à la maison depuis le début de saison (5 victoires en 5 matches). Extraits.

"Saint-Etienne, Geoffroy Guichard, ça m'évoque beaucoup de souvenirs, même si j'étais très jeune à la fin des années 70. Mais évidemment, tout le monde en parlait. Au début des années 80, c'était le club phare, un peu le PSG d'aujourd'hui, avec d'autres valeurs, avec d'autres moyens. Les Verts, ça a bercé mon enfance. J'ai beaucoup vu d'images, j'ai entendu beaucoup de chansons qui ont aussi dicté mon enfance. Lorsque j'ai démarré le foot à 6 ans, très tôt, j'ai eu un maillot de Saint-Etienne à mettre sur mes épaules aux entraînements. Le fameux Manufrance, Le Coq sportif…

Les Verts, c'est beaucoup de souvenirs, un club mythique de notre patrimoine footballistique. Bon, aujourd'hui, un club se trouvant en Ligue 2… Je n'ai pas les yeux de l'enfant qui rêvait devant ces images. C'est aussi de la fierté de pouvoir mener un club, une équipe à jouer dans ce stade-là, face à cette équipe-là. Avec vraiment l'ambition de leur rendre la tâche difficile, d'aller essayer de prendre quelque chose, au moins un point. D’autant plus que c'est un stade que je n'ai jamais vu de match là-bas. Je l'ai visité, mais vide. Là, je vais voir un match de très près. C'est top !

Les Verts ont remporté tous leurs matches à domicile, on n’a pris qu’un point à l’extérieur ? Quand on présente le match comme ça, il est totalement déséquilibré, évidemment, même si la dernière journée, ils ont perdu, nous, on a gagné. Donc, on leur a pris trois points (sourire). Mais non, c'est une équipe qui, pour l'instant, est leader. Sans l'ombre d'un doute, en fait ! C'est vrai qu'ils ont eu cette défaite avec un très bon match des Guingampais. J'ai vu que le coach de Saint-Etienne a attribué ça à la thèse de l'accident. On va faire en sorte que ce soit peut-être un peu plus qu'un accident. Mais on sait que ça va être très, très difficile.

Nous, c'est vrai qu'on n'a pas pris de points à l'extérieur encore. Enfin si, un à Metz tout de même. Mais on a fait cependant des matchs très cohérents à l'extérieur. Et dans la dynamique des récents matchs, j'ai bon espoir qu'on obtienne quelque chose. En affichant la solidité démontrée au moins sur les deux derniers matchs. Il va falloir qu'on soit très discipliné, très rigoureux tout au long du match. Mais effectivement, sans trop se renier, si on doit leur poser des problèmes, c'est aussi en pouvant utiliser le ballon de la meilleure des manières. Et ça, on a quand même posé des problèmes à plusieurs adversaires. Même à l'extérieur.

Même si l'ambiance sera tout autre, j'espère vraiment que mes joueurs vont faire preuve de grande personnalité pour pouvoir leur poser des problèmes dans notre utilisation du ballon. Parce que c'est une équipe qui l'utilise bien. Ils savent aussi faire le dos rond, ils savent défendre. Mais je pense qu'ils préfèrent avoir le ballon que défendre malgré tout. Il y aura des moments charnières dans ce match qu'il faudra bien négocier. Mais il faudra qu'on soit très bons dans ce que l'on fait, dans ce qu'on essaie de faire avec le ballon. Et j'espère vraiment qu'on pourra le démontrer demain.

Si c’est un match bonus ? Ce qui serait bonus, c'est si on obtenait un résultat. Parce que peu d'équipes ont pris des points contre Saint-Etienne. Dunkerque et Guingamp en ont pris trois. Et Metz en a pris un. Après, pour tous les autres, ça a été difficile. Mais là, je ne le considère pas comme un match de gala ou de bonus. Non, c'est ce que j'ai déjà dit aux joueurs et que je vais répéter en fin de journée. Pour nous, notre premier enjeu, c'est de continuer et de valider cette victoire enfin obtenue contre Boulogne. Ce premier clean sheet. L'enjeu est là, c'est de continuer. Ça tombe sur un déplacement à Geoffroy-Guichard. Evidemment, ce n'est pas chose aisée. Mais est-ce qu'il y a vraiment des matchs plus faciles que d'autres ? Je n'en suis pas certain.

Quand on présente comme ça un déplacement à Saint-Etienne qui presque survole le championnat, ce n'est pas facile. Peu d'équipes ont pris des points aux Verts mais il y en a certaines qui ont réussi. Et le football, encore une fois, permet ça. Et donc, pourquoi pas l'avoir à Saint-Etienne ? En tout cas, mon discours sera là. Encore une fois, avec beaucoup d'humilité. Pas question de se prendre pour d'autres sous prétexte qu'on a gagné contre Boulogne. Pas du tout. Mais je sais qu'on peut leur poser des problèmes. Je sais que ça va être difficile. Ça va être très difficile par moments. Mais tout est possible. Et je veux qu'on y aille avec cette ambition-là. Mais ce n'est pas le match de gala, le match bonus. Pas du tout.

Moi, j'aimerais qu'on continue et qu'on enchaîne après cette victoire contre Boulogne, acquise dans la difficulté malgré tout. Mais si on peut aller prendre au moins un point dans la difficulté à Saint-Etienne, ça ne fera que renforcer notre dynamique. Et là, je vois une simple victoire contre Boulogne, tout ce que ça peut amener, engendrer comme soulagement, comme bien-être, même si avant ça, malgré les défaites, l'ambiance était bonne, le travail était bien, le climat, tout était positif. Mais là, on voit de suite qu'une seule victoire, même difficilement acquise, ça nous fait sauter deux places déjà. Et on veut continuer là-dessus."

#2479. Sports club members becoming less tolerant of homosexuality

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

Acceptance of homosexuality in Dutch sports clubs has declined significantly in the last generation, according to a survey of attitudes by the Mulier Institute.

The organisation found that 66% of people had no objection to a fellow member of a sports club being gay, while in 2008 the figure was 85%.

Just over half (55%) of respondents said they had no problem sharing a shower with a gay team-mate of the same sex, which also represented a drop from 73% in 2008.

The Mulier Institute said the findings reflected a social trend that has also been seen in schools, where the proportion of boys aged between 12 and 16 with a positive view of homosexuality has dropped from 40% to 22% since 2021.

The Dutch Olympic Committee NOC*NSF said the findings were “highly regrettable” and pledged to step up its efforts to make sport more inclusive.

But the John Blankenstein Foundation, which campaigns for LGBT emancipation in sport, said it had noticed declining enthusiasm for its workshops and growing numbers of parents who voiced objections.

Karin Blankenstein, who set up the organisation in 2008, told NOS: “I get the sensitivity around transgender people in sport at the top level, but this is about amateur sport.”

#2478. Saint-Etienne, ça fait plus rêver que les autres équipes

Auteur du but égalisateur des Lavallois contre les Verts à Francis Le Basser lors de la première journée de la saison dernière, le Lavallois Ethan Clavreul (20 ans) se réjouit d'être dans le groupe lavallois qui débarquera demain soir dans le Chaudron, comme il l'a expliqué en conférence de presse.

"La saison passée, j'avais vu à la télé le match à Saint-Etienne. Franchement, c'est beau à voir. Même quand tu regardes leurs matchs, il y a toujours une ambiance, il y a une ferveur, il y a un truc. Après moi, quand je rentre sur le terrain, je fais abstraction des tribunes. Peut-être qu'il y aura du bruit, il y aura du monde. Tant que tu fais un bon match, que tu joues, c'est le principal. Franchement, contre Saint-Etienne à Laval, c'était un sentiment particulier parce que c'est mon premier match, mon premier but. Mais là, ça fait 6 gros mois que je suis dans le monde du foot pro. Je sais ce que c'est les ambiances. Franchement, oui, ça peut être impressionnant au début, mais une fois que l'échauffement commence, je suis dans ma bulle et je ne regarde pas trop autour.

Sportivement, quand on affronte une équipe qui n'a pas lâché un point à domicile, comment arrive-t-on à se dire qu'il y a quelque chose à aller chercher là-bas ? On sait qu'on a des armes, ils ont des faiblesses. Ils ont des armes, on a aussi des faiblesses, comme toute équipe. Après, on l'a bien vu, vendredi dernier contre Boulogne, si on est un peu moins beau, ce n'est pas grave. L'objectif, c'est de gagner des matchs. En vrai, on a des armes qui peuvent les embêter comme eux ont des armes pour nous embêter. Je pense que si on est un peu plus mordants qu'eux, on peut les surprendre là-bas et faire un bon coup dans la tête et au classement aussi.

Je pense que le fait qu’ils aient perdu leur dernier match, ça a dû les piquer. Je pense qu'ils se sont dit que la saison n'était pas finie. Ça fait une petite piqûre de rappel. C'est toujours bien pour toute équipe qui gagne beaucoup de matchs à domicile ou à l'extérieur. Nous, on sait qu'ils ont perdu. On sait qu'ils vont être revanchards pour leur public parce que pour eux, c'est interdit de perdre deux fois d'affilée. Nous, on sait qu'on vient d'avoir une victoire à domicile. Nous, on a envie d'emmener le public avec nous. On les a emmenés un peu à Boulogne. Si on gagne à Saint-Etienne, je pense que contre Montpellier, il y aura un peu plus de monde dans les tribunes et un peu plus de bruit.

Quand je savais que Saint-Etienne était encore dans notre championnat, quand on l'a vu, que nous restions aussi dans ce championnat-là, je me dis que ce match-là, à l'extérieur, il faut que j'y aille. L'année dernière, je ne l'ai pas fait. J'étais dégoûté. Toute ma famille avait fait le déplacement. Quand je les appelle, je dis que je ne suis pas dans le groupe. Ce n'était pas extraordinaire. Donc, cette année, je me suis dit que toute la saison, je vais prendre le match par match. Saint-Etienne, ça fait toujours un peu plus rêver que les autres équipes. Ce n'est pas pour autant que je néglige toutes les autres équipes. C'est que Saint-Etienne, il y a une ferveur, il y a un truc. Personnellement, jouer des matchs avec beaucoup d'ambiance, beaucoup de monde au stade, des gros stades comme ça… Oui, ça fait toujours un petit truc dans sa tête !"

#2476. Top five football betting tips and tricks

Football is the world’s most famous sport, and it is the sport of choice of a great many sports punters as well. Especially popular in Europe, betting on football can be an incredibly profitable enterprise, so long as you know how to do it.

Getting started on football betting is far from easy, though. This form of gambling requires dedication, investment, and persistence. As you gain experience, your intuition will improve and you will be able to make more correct predictions.

But experience is not the only thing that can help you become a better football punter. In fact, you can substantially improve your betting strategy by simply following some basic football betting tips.

You might find shopping around boring and unhelpful, but you just have to do it when you search sites for football betting. As you know, the higher the odds, the bigger the payout. What you may have missed, however, is that odds are different at each bookmaker. Having said that, shopping around for the best odds is sensible, as it rewards you with bigger payouts.

In this line of thought, sticking to one bookmaker is virtually pointless. Even if the bookie of your choosing offers the best odds for a number of matches, it is highly unlikely that their odds will remain the best for long.

An obvious one, we know. And yet, you will be surprised by the number of football bettors who wager on football, but do not follow the sport. So, why is it important to follow the action closely?

First of all, by watching football matches, you can take part in the most exciting football betting market – the live betting market. What is more important, however, is that following the action provides an invaluable insight into teams and players’ style, condition, and determination – important factors  to consider when placing a bet.

As you watch more and more games, you will improve your analytic abilities. In time, you will be able to make more accurate predictions about the outcome of different matches, which, of course,  will result in placing more winning bets.

As you know, odds are based on the probability of occurrence of a certain outcome; the higher the odds, the more unlikely it is for that outcome to actually occur. But what happens when the odds offered by the bookmaker represent a probability that is lower than the actual probability of an outcome occurring? In such a situation, you can place what is called a value bet.

If you want to become a professional football punter, you should always search for value when betting, as it leads to huge profits

Favoured by beginners in particular, accumulators are a type of football wager which allows you to bet on as many fixtures as you like. Most commonly, you will be asked to choose a match winner, but as the market is diverse, you may be offered a number of other betting options. What makes accumulators an appealing betting market is the huge potential payout – here, you can stake as little as £1 and have the potential to win more than £500,000, depending on the bookmaker and the number of selected fixtures.

If you find accumulators’ payouts too good to be true, you are right. As a matter of fact, there is a reason for the odds to be set so high – actually winning an accumulator bet, especially one which involves a wide selection of matches, is nearly impossible. You see, all you need for your whole wager to lose is one wrong prediction. For example, if you bet on 10 matches and just one of them goes wrong, your entire accumulator is lost.

To make money by betting on football, avoid betting on accumulators – the risk is simply not worth it.

Finally, if you want to be a successful football punter, learn how not to mix emotions with betting. This is easier said than done, though.

Whenever your favourite team plays, you want them to win, regardless of how (im)possible that may be. If you do not learn how to leave emotions out of betting, however, you are highly likely to put money on your favourite team even if they have no chance of winning the match. Conversely, should you base your bets on hard facts and not on emotions, you increase your chances to win.

Our advice – avoid betting on fixtures that involve one of your favourite teams, that is the easiest way to keep emotions at bay.

Joshua Kimmich described Bayern Munich's 2-1 victory over Mainz 05 as an "Arbeitssieg", a hard-fought victory, after the final whistle. The midfielder acknowledged that his side had not produced a spectacular performance but was satisfied with the result and defensive stability.

"It was a hard fought, but deserved win today, we didn't allow many chances for the opponents", Kimmich explained. He also highlighted the importance of avoiding an equaliser before half-time after Mainz had briefly thought they had found the net.

The Bayern captain's opening goal carried particular emotional significance due to a personal family connection. “The goal was highly emotional for me due to what happend with my father. I instantly thought about him after scoring”, he revealed.

Kimmich also praised the atmosphere within the Bayern dressing room, highlighting the progress made under the current coaching staff over the past two to three years.

"What has come together with the coaching staff over the last two or three years is special," he explained, adding that there is greater stability within the club and a stronger sense of togetherness. He believes this unity has become particularly important during Vincent Kompany's current absence.

Finally, Kimmich expressed his delight at Harry Kane's continued commitment to Bayern, praising both the striker's character and his influence on the team.

"He is a great guy and one of the reasons for the fantastic atmosphere in our dressing room. He wins the Ballon d'Or and still only thinks about the team. He is very special," Kimmich said.

A late equaliser for Fortuna Sittard in De Kuip proved costly for Feyenoord as PSV and Twente racked up huge victories to move within two points of the Eredivisie leaders.

Fortuna equalised with four minutes left when Shiloh ’t Zand, on loan from Feyenoord, was allowed to run through to the penalty area and beat Tjark Ernst with a low shot.

Feyenoord coach Giovanni van Bronckhorst made no effort to hide his disappointment. “We know that Fortuna are the kind of team who can score from the few attacks they have,” he said. “If you don’t score the goals, things like this can happen.”

Twente recorded the biggest win of the weekend with a 9-0 demolition of Excelsior in Rotterdam, with Wout Weghorst scoring four times and Sondre Ørjasaeter notching up five assists.

Excelsior collapsed following the fifth-minute dismissal of Nesto Groen for a reckless high challenge. The home side were 5-0 down at half-time, with Weghorst claiming a hat-trick in the opening 33 minutes.

PSV bagged seven goals in a one-sided encounter with bottom club Willem II, with all the goals coming from different scorers including Ricardo Pepi, who came on in the second half after a six-week absence through injury.

ADO went down 1-0 in Zwolle, with PEC’s Dylan Mbayo scoring the only goal with a first-time finish on the stroke of half-time.

The match between Cambuur Leeuwarden and Go Ahead Eagles was suspended in the 82nd minute after visiting fans threw fireworks onto the pitch.

The clubs agreed with the KNVB not to play the final minutes and allow Cambuur’s 4-0 advantage to stand as the final result.

Go Ahead’s director Jan-Willem van Dop said the club would take action against those responsible: “I am ashamed on behalf a small group of supporters who ruined things for the club and a large hare of the fans in the away end,” he said.

L’arbitro non è obbligato a interrompere automaticamente il gioco ogni volta che un calciatore viene colpito dal pallone. Lo deve fare quando ritiene che ci sia un infortunio grave. Per questo, secondo la mia valutazione, Colombo ha fatto bene a lasciar proseguire l’azione.

#2467. Opinion: The morality of football

Yesterday evening, Selecao correspondent Nathan Motz accepted a unique opportunity to appear as a guest on the Moral Maze – a BBC radio broadcast dealing with matters of moral and ethical significance.

Is football a moral force for good? That was the question of the day, and each of the four guests were asked to discuss various aspects of the debate from financial fairplay to club takeovers by foreign entities to racism.

Nathan’s segment was concerned with whether football has transcendent moral value, and what that is exactly. Is there a greater significance to the game we are watching, or is it merely 22 humans kicking a leather ball into a net?

Join Nathan as he elaborates on the most significant takeaways of what was a fascinating discussion on the sport and morality in general.

Football, for some, may be the most poignant reminder of the moral universe in which we live. While debate has raged for centuries regarding the precise origin of moral absolutes and how humans ought to live, it is simply unavoidable to say humanity is often troubled by events surrounding the game we all love so dearly.

In not so distant history, every normal act of human life would have been assigned some degree of moral significance. Every action had inherently moral indicators - for good or for evil. Today, postmodern views of morality dominate. These describe morality as an entirely subjective dimension. In other words, it cannot be pinned down exactly whether behaviors are objectively good or bad. We can only find fault with someone else’s interpretation based on their own unique moral code. Morality is relative to the individual or society.

Yet last night’s dialogue concerning football and human behavior was filled with moral accusations and claims which strongly suggested humans are not content with relativist morality. Indeed, we are at times frightened by and disgusted with what we observe in and around football. Our sense of moral justice is deeply disturbed. But with respect to what exactly?

In responding to the panelist’s questions, I observed a familiar paradigm. Each time I have discussed any aspect of moral behavior, the speculation humans are good or at worst morally neutral animals continually reemerges. This widely accepted presupposition is then followed shortly by disgust at some aspect of human behavior. Almost as if to ask “why are all these good humans behaving so badly?” while ignoring the very real implications of this line of inquiry. A question rendered unanswerable by the insistent a priori assumptions from which it was derived.

If humans are intrinsically beneficent, conscientious, fair, humble, and honest then why is football rife with racism, misogyny, greed, disenfranchisement, lies, cheating, violence, and the hypocrisy to deny the occurrence of any such behaviors except when a rival club is the guilty party? Behaviors which are perpetrated by individual supporters, fanatical groups, club administrations, corporate bodies such as FIFA, political entities, nations, and even the players themselves. No one participating faction is guiltless.

To so blithely sidestep more sufficient explanations for the empirical reality of wickedness in football and in human behavior more generally is not just factually incorrect. It undermines real attempts to understand the root of these behaviors where they might be dealt with more effectively.

To listen to The Moral Maze episode entitled “Football: a moral force for good?” produced by BBC radio and featuring PortuGOAL’s Seleção correspondent Nathan Motz click on this link

Furthermore, in good faith, how successful is a response against heinous evil when those perpetrating it are regarded as behaving in accord with their unique moral ambitions? Humans refuse to accept their indisputably objective moral lacking at a fundamental level. This restricts us from ever doing any more than “stamping out” the alleged impropriety of others – while we ourselves may readily engage in these very same behaviors. Moral relativism cannot account for the moral outrage emanating from all corners of the football world. Is Qatar wrong to perpetrate human rights atrocities or is it allowable because their culture is indifferent? Is hooliganism objectively wrong, or only when someone else’s club is responsible?

The overlooking of our own failings while lashing out at others for theirs is quintessential to the morally fallen nature of humanity itself. Football tells all. There are not good people and bad people. There are only people who both willfully and unintentionally transgress the moral code embedded in each of our hearts. A moral law which can only have ever been given by a moral Lawgiver. Otherwise, it is truly your word against mine as it pertains to the moral validity of all behaviors - even those which may deeply violate your conscience.

Football generates an episodic, semantic, and imagistic description of universal archetypes: good and evil, sacrifice and greed, cruelty and mercy, hero and anti-hero. It speaks to every arc of life’s plotline: tragedy, comedy, victory, defeat, the finite nature of our circumstances. It is one of the preeminent existential dialects alongside religion, visual art, written and spoken language, music, and theatre. Football plucks a primal chord.

As metaphysics have been curtailed to just physics in the modern secular West, football has only elevated our awareness of the often dire moral predicaments of our species. Yet it is clear the moral conversation does not originate on a football pitch. The pitch is but a grand stage upon which moral themes may be observed and critiqued, a mirror held in front of our own soul.

The desire for ultimate purpose, for meaning beyond the material realm, never disappeared in modernity. Arguably, it has only become more desperate as the outcry for ultimate significance comes under more scrutiny and criticism by those who constrain ontological explanations to those accounted for by physics. The severity of the mental health crisis in the West is in no small part explained by the terrifying existential awareness we are cut adrift in an abstract sea of meaningless, hopeless wandering between two oblivions - that before mortal life began and that at mortal life’s end. Let football be one voice which reassures you - there is ultimate meaning and good reasons why football so unsettles our craving for justice.

In a palpable, unavoidable way, football and supporter communities across Europe are encountering moral quandaries. In Portugal, age-old debates between FC Porto and Benfica have routinely been haunted by, in many cases, farcical and cynical hypocrisy. Throughout its history, the FPF has been susceptible to showing partiality to a club, player, or style which ultimately degrades the performance of our beloved Seleção. Deny it all we like, and not one aspect of this reality is changed.

In conclusion, whatever your thoughts or beliefs on the subject of moral significance, its origin, the meaning of life, and how football draws that out, I hope it will be beneficial to examine these matters more deeply. In my zeal, I admit I have not always been prudent to initiate these discussions in a way which respects thoughtful disagreement. It is my hope this effort to bring that discussion to Portuguese football will be well-received and carefully pondered. But I will contentedly listen to those followers who may be genuinely unhappy or unsupportive of this or any other idea about which I write.

The readers of this website and the larger Portuguese football loving world are my people. It is a joy and a responsibility I take seriously whenever I have the opportunity to raise important questions about the Selecao, the game, and our lives in general.

#2466. Dual passports issue continues to plague Dutch-based footballers

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

Dutch-born footballers who were called up to play for former colonies such as Suriname and Indonesia are still in limbo over their national status, six months after the “passportgate” crisis first flared up.

Suriname’s parliament passed a law in September enabling players who are selected for the national team to gain citizenship automatically.

But the Dutch immigration service IND said the law passed in Suriname made no difference to the rules on dual nationality in the Netherlands.

“The fact that Surinamese nationality has been obtained by legal means is not enough on its own to qualify for one of the exceptional categories under our law,” a spokesman for the Dutch justice ministry told broadcaster NOS.

The law was intended to settle the question of dual nationality for footballers, which arose last March when NAC Breda challenged the eligibility of Go Ahead Eagles defender Dean James.

James took Indonesian citizenship last year after accepting an invitation to play for the national team, but under Dutch dual nationality rules he was required to give up his original passport.

The Dutch football association KNVB launched an investigation into the employment status of players who were called up by countries such as Suriname, Cape Verde and Indonesia. In the meantime it arranged for Dutch-based players to be given temporary work permits so they could keep playing for their clubs.

Losing Dutch nationality also stripped them of their EU citizenship, meaning they would need to obtain visas and meet stricter criteria, such as an annual salary threshold of €600,000, to be eligible to play in the Eredivisie.

Several players have passed up invitations to join the Surinamese squad because of the uncertainty, including Danilho Doekhi of Lazio, Milan van Ewijk of Coventry City and Jayden Oosterwolde of Fenerbahçe.

National team manager Henk Fraser said it was “inexplicable” that players’ livelihoods were being damaged by their desire to play international football.

“Both countries have to get together and clarify the situation,” he told NOS. “This is a straightforward case of people following their hearts and wanting to play for Suriname, but not knowing where they stand.”

Some players said they felt misled by the Surinamese football association, which told them they were playing for the national team on “sports passports” that allowed them to remain Dutch citizens.

Former Feyenoord winger Jean-Paul Boëtius said he only discovered after he was first called up for Suriname last year that he had inadvertently renounced his Dutch citizenship as a result.

Shortly afterwards he received a letter from the city council in Rotterdam telling him to hand in his Dutch passport, which he had recently renewed, because it was no longer valid.

“I now have a sticker in my Surinamese passport that says I have a residency permit allowing me to live in the Netherlands,” he told NOS. “But that’s only for a year. It’s a very difficult situation. I want to become Dutch again. I was born and brought up here.”

#2463. Briançon prépare sa reconversion

Valeureux et heureux capitaine d'un Pau FC 5e de L2 et restant sur une série de 5 succès consécutifs avant de recevoir ce soir les Canaris 5 semaines avant d'accueillir les Verts, l'ancien défenseur stéphanois Anthony Briançon, qui fêtera ses 32 ans dans 3 semaines, a évoqué en début de semaine sa reconversion sur son compte LinkedIn.

"À la base, j’ai créé un compte LinkedIn parce que c’était un exercice demandé pendant ma formation à l'EM Lyon Business School . Je ne pensais pas forcément que ce réseau aurait un véritable intérêt pour moi. Et puis, en avançant dans ma réflexion sur l’après-carrière, j’ai compris quelque chose : préparer la suite ne commence pas le jour où l’on arrête de jouer.

Aujourd’hui, je suis encore pleinement footballeur. J’ai encore des objectifs, des matchs à jouer et beaucoup de choses à vivre sur les terrains. Mais en parallèle, je me forme au scouting, à la data et au métier de directeur sportif. Et pourquoi pas, à terme, passer aussi mes diplômes d’entraîneur.

Après des années passées dans les vestiaires, sur les terrains et au contact de nombreux entraîneurs, dirigeants et joueurs, forcément, on développe un regard différent sur le football. Être capitaine pendant plusieurs années m’a aussi beaucoup appris sur l’humain, l’écoute et l’importance de faire avancer un groupe ensemble.

On apprend à observer un joueur au-delà de ses qualités techniques, à comprendre sa personnalité, ce qu’il peut apporter humainement à un groupe, mais aussi l’importance du partage, de la transmission et des relations que l’on construit au quotidien. Mais l’expérience du terrain ne suffit pas. C’est justement pour ça que j’ai envie de la compléter aujourd’hui

Par de nouvelles compétences, de nouvelles méthodes et une autre manière d’analyser le jeu. Je ne sais pas encore exactement où cette deuxième partie de carrière me mènera. En revanche, je sais dans quelle direction j’ai envie d’avancer. Et je trouve finalement assez logique de commencer à la construire pendant que je joue encore. La carrière de joueur a une fin. La passion du football, beaucoup moins."

#2460. Feyenoord facing fireworks fine after 3-0 loss to Internazionale

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

Feyenoord are facing a heavy fine from European football authorities after fireworks were let off during their Champions League defeat by Internazionale.

Referee Glenn Nyberg suspended the match for several minutes after flares were fired onto the roof from outside De Kuip stadium, damaging the structure. Police later made two arrests.

“It’s obviously not right and it’ll be another fine,” coach Giovanni van Bronckhorst said wearily after the game, which ended in a comprehensive 3-0 defeat for his team.

One flare landed on the pitch despite the club taking measures such as hanging nets from the gantries to stop objects being thrown from the stands. “They’re letting off fireworks from outside now,” Van Bronckhorst said. “What do you do about that?”

Uefa are likely to hit Feyenoord with a fine and could order the club to close parts of the stadium for their next home matches in the competition, against Porto on November 24 and RB Leipzig on January 27.

The club also have a suspended penalty hanging over them from last October, when they were fined €60,000 after fans let off fireworks during the match against Greek club Panathinaikos.

On the pitch, Feyenoord were undone by a classic display of Italian defending and counter-attacking, as Internazionale sat back after taking an eighth-minute lead through Yann Bisseck.

Francesco Pio Esposito headed in a second early in the second half, just as Feyenoord were pushing for an equaliser, before Piotr Zielinski made sure of the win with a shot from 20 metres.

Captain Luciano Valente said the result was “more than deserved” and the visitors had shown more “quality”.

“This defeat was harder to take than the one against Barcelona,” he told Ziggo Sport. “Because we thought going in that we could get something, but we just weren’t good enough.”

Van Bronckhorst said he was not surprised by the difference in class between the teams. “They scored the first goal out of nothing,” he said. “And Inter know better what the game needs at every stage.

“Their players have a lot of experience, they’ve been in the final twice in the last four years. So we’re at a disadvantage.”

#2456. PSV edged out by sharper Club Brugge in Champions League defeat

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results

PSV Eindhoven endured a frustrating night in the Champions League as their defensive frailties were exposed in a 2-1 defeat by Club Brugge.

PSV started full of attacking intent and should have taken an early lead when Ivan Perisic put Sergiño Dest clean through on goal, but the defender’s shot was too close to goalkeeper Yann Sommer.

Instead it was the visitors who took the lead with their first real attack when Nicolo Tresoldi headed in a teasing cross from Joaquin Seys.

PSV equalised early in the second half as an outstanding pass by Sven Mijnans into the path Lutsharel Geertruida allowed the defender to charge to the byline. His pullback found its way to Kodai Sano, whose shot took a deflection off Seys into the net.

But five minutes later PSV were caught out as another razor-sharp pass by Seys released Carlos Forbs in space to net the winner.

The home side refused to give up, but were unable to find a way past Sommer in the closing stages as the Belgian side closed out the win.

Team captain Mauro Junior admitted that PSV had been undone by shortcomings at the back. “We had a lot of opportunities in the first half but we didn’t score,” he told Ziggo Sport.

“We want to attack and score goals, but we weren’ well organised today in defence. We need to keep our heads up and look forward to the next game.”

The result leaves PSV in 24th place, just above the elimination line, ahead of their next match away to Real Madrid on November 24, with four points from their opening four matches.

#2455. Surveillez Houdayer !

Depuis leur écrasant succès (11-1) contre Ecotay-Moingt (R3) il y a un an en Coupe de France, jamais les Verts n'ont été donnés autant favoris avant une rencontre que ce soir contre Laval selon les bookmakers.

Ayant à coeur de se racheter après leur défaite à Guingamp, les leaders stéphanois ont remporté tous leurs matches à domicile cette saison tandis que les antépénultièmes Tango n'ont pris qu'un petit point hors de leurs bases.

Le Stade Lavallois, qui n'a jamais gagné à Geoffroy (mais y a subi 16 défaites et arraché 5 nuls), va débarquer diminué dans le Chaudron car il sera notamment privé de 3 avant-centres (tous blessés) : Corentin Fatou, Trévis Dago et Abou Kanté.

Mais les Mayennais ne seront pas complètement démunis offensivement. L'ASSE devra en particulier se méfier de leur meilleur buteur Mathys Houdayer (21 ans), qui a claqué 4 pions (contre Nantes, le Red Star, Pau et Boulogne).

"Artiste et artificier de ce début de saison tango" dixit Ouest-France il y a 8 jours, il a été désigné 6 fois meilleur joueur lavallois cette saison par l'entraîneur de l'équipe d'en face selon Foot National (seuls le Rémois Yahia Fofana et le Nancéien Ilyes Housni ont recueilli davantage de suffrages).

Passeur décisif lors des 2 matches de barrage contre Rouen en mai dernier, le natif de Laval, qui a prolongé son contrat avec son club formateur jusqu'en 2029, a mis en exergue sa polyvalence il y a 2 mois dans le quotidien régional le plus diffusé de France.

"Un attaquant, ça fonctionne avec la confiance. J’ai celle du coach, des supporters, de mes coéquipiers et de mon premier but contre Nantes. J’aime beaucoup être sur le côté. Mais en début de saison, le coach m’a dit qu’il essaierait de m’utiliser le plus possible en faux n°9, 9, 10. pourquoi pas 8 de temps en temps. En 10, je peux prendre des espaces, avoir des joueurs qui fixent les défenseurs, et me laissent le temps de me retourner. C’est un peu régal pour moi."

#2454. Lazio, Zaccagni fuori e Tavares in dubbio: emergenza a sinistra

Gattuso spera di recuperare Tavares all’ultimo momento; Pedraza e Luca Pellegrini sono già in preallarme.

Zaccagni è fuori e Nuno Tavares è in dubbio per un affaticamento al flessore: la Lazio affronta un’emergenza sulla sinistra in vista della gara al Maradona. Secondo quanto riportato da Il Messaggero, Tavares non si è allenato nell’ultima seduta e Gattuso spera di recuperarlo all’ultimo momento.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Dal resto del gruppo arrivano segnali migliori. Cataldi non ha riportato conseguenze dopo il suo primo ingresso. Marusic e Rovella, dopo oltre due mesi, sono pronti a tornare in panchina al Maradona, secondo Il Messaggero.

Marusic risponderà anche alla convocazione della sua Nazionale durante la sosta. Per Gattuso, i due rientri rappresentano una notizia positiva mentre restano da gestire le assenze sulla sinistra.

#2453. "A huge dream": Jobe Bellingham grateful to Kovač after earning England call-up

Borussia Dortmund kept pace with Bayern Munich at the top of the Bundesliga table after overcoming Elversberg 2-1. Following the final whistle, Felix Nmecha reflected on a challenging afternoon for BVB:

"I think I struck the ball well twice today," Nmecha said with a laugh. "We knew Elversberg were a good footballing side, and they certainly didn't make things easy for us. In the end, you have to fight, that's what you have to do, and then you win."

Asked about the ongoing title battle with Bayern Munich, Nmecha played down its significance at this stage of the season.

"It's a long season, and the battle with Bayern isn't decisive for us just yet. We're taking it one game at a time. That's all we can do right now."

Meanwhile, Jobe Bellingham had plenty of reasons to celebrate following his selection for the England national team. The Dortmund midfielder credited Kovač and his coaching staff for helping him reach this milestone.

"I'm really grateful to my coach Niko Kovač. My development and the fact that I was selected for the national team are largely thanks to him and the coaching staff. I'm really happy, and I want to keep working hard and developing further," Bellingham explained.

The 20-year-old also spoke about the possibility of sharing the pitch with his brother Jude at international level – a prospect that would fulfil a childhood dream.

"Playing alongside my brother Jude for England is a huge dream. I can't stop smiling about it. We dreamed about this when we played together in the garden. I don't want to jinx anything because it hasn't happened yet, but I'm really looking forward to it, and I know he is too," he added.

Xavi Hernandez has his first win as Netherlands head coach as a Mexx Meerdink double led Oranje to a 2-1 win over Serbia in the Nations League.

Xavi made two changes from the side that drew with Germany as Mexx Meerdink replaced the injured Brian Brobbey up front while Joey Veerman came in for Quinten Timber.

Netherlands dominated from the start but they were forced into an early change when Cody Gakpo suffered a knock and Ruben van Bommel came on.

After half an hour, Crysencio Summerville was clipped in the box and Meerdink made it 1-0 from the penalty spot. It was almost 2-0 before the break but Denzel Dumfries could not convert when through on goal.

Serbia made a double change at the break with Luka Jovic and Dusan Tadic coming on. A minute into the second half, the change worked as Jovic was given space in the box to net after Netherlands failed to clear the ball.

Netherlands went in search of the lead again but Tijjani Reijnders blazed a huge chance over while Ruben van Bommel hit the post. Meerdink did eventually make it 2-1 with a lovely finish after a quick 1-2 with substitute Guus Til.

There was to be no hattrick for Meerdink as he was brought off and Netherlands played the final ten minutes with Til through the middle.

Serbia pressed for a late equaliser but the Netherlands had a golden chance to make it 3-1 when Til found himself in on goal. He had Summerville and Van Bommel to his left but overhit the pass and the later’s finish was too weak to find the net.

The Netherlands held on for the victory which takes them to the top of the group with a clash against Greece next on Thursday.

#2450. U17 : On Aytone puis on bétonne

Vous avez pu suivre cette rencontre en direct sur notre forum grâce à nos envoyés spéciaux Stéphanois et MachuPicchu42 : les U17 ont battu leurs homologues clermontois sur le plus petit des scores cet après-midi à Aimé-Jacquet.

Lors d'une première période guère emballante, seul l'ailier gauche Naël Dani a mis en danger des Auvergnats un peu plus entreprenants mais bien muselés par la charnière centrale Naoufan Mnemoi - Lucas Dagbo.

Juste après l'heure de jeu, sur une bonne passe du numéro 8 Sabri Sercu, Isaak Saydi a fait une percée supersonique avant de délivrer une passe décisive à Gibril Aytone, entré en jeu dès la 32e minute à la place de Léo Fayolle.

Les Verts ont beaucoup souffert pour conserver leur court mais très précieux avantage. Ils ont heureusement pu compter sur la maladresse des visiteurs, qui ont eu en outre la malchance de trouver la barre transversale d'Andrea Laugier à la 87e minute.

La compo : Laugier - Fayolle (Aytone, 33'), Mnemoi, Dagbo, Bufferne - Diakité (Giangreco, 61'), Sercu, Thibaudat - Seydi, Kimpioka, Dani (Tiambo, 90').

La réaction de David Le Moal : "En première mi-temps, on a eu quelques sorties de balle intéressantes, mais par contre il y a eu un manque de coordination et de connexion entre tous les joueurs de l'équipe. Je trouve qu'on a manqué d'énergie aussi. La seconde mi-temps a été encore plus laborieuse. On n'a fait quasiment que défendre après la pause. On a eu quelques contres sur lesquels on a mal joué les coups. On a beaucoup subi même en étant bien en place derrière. C'est dommage qu'on ait concédé autant d'occasions alors qu'on avait décidé de fermer un peu le jeu. Le résultat est bon, on a gagné. Mais en ce qui concerne le contenu de ce match, il n'y a pas grand chose de positif à retenir. On a décroché une victoire sans prendre de but avec beaucoup de U16 qui ne jouaient pas à leut poste donc ça fait quand même quelques satisfactions."

Dopo la sosta i biancocelesti ospiteranno il Lecce e andranno a Torino; dal 6 dicembre inizierà la serie di sfide più impegnative.

La Lazio di Gattuso si prepara a un calendario in salita: dopo la partita di domenica e due impegni alla ripresa dalla sosta, tra il 6 dicembre e il 10 gennaio arriveranno sfide con Atalanta, Roma, Frosinone, Inter, Fiorentina e Como. Secondo quanto riportato da Il Messaggero, Finora il tecnico ha affrontato sette delle ultime dieci squadre di Serie A. La prossima serie di gare offrirà un quadro diverso.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Alla ripresa del campionato, i biancocelesti riceveranno il Lecce all’Olimpico. Seguirà il viaggio a Torino per affrontare i granata: due partite che precederanno il tratto più impegnativo del programma, ricostruito dal Messaggero.

La sequenza comincerà il 6 dicembre con l’Atalanta in casa. Sarri è indicato alla guida dei bergamaschi, pur con un’incertezza sulla sua permanenza in panchina fino a quella partita. Poi arriverà il derby, con i tifosi attesi di nuovo a riempire la Curva Nord.

Il 18 dicembre la Lazio giocherà sul campo del Frosinone di Alvini, allo Stirpe. In seguito è prevista la finale a San Siro contro l’Inter, nel periodo che comprende anche la Supercoppa.

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 11 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 10 of the Eredivisie ...

An injury-hit Netherlands side battled back to draw 2-2 against Greece in the Nations League.

Xavi was forced into changes with Cody Gakpo and Jan Paul van Hecke dropping out of the squad. Tijjani Reijnders and Ryan Gravenberch also dropped to the bench with Quinten Timber, Guus Til, Jurrien Timber and Ruben van Bommel starting.

Greece have been in good form recently and their confidence was clear from the start. They had a goal disallowed for offside before Fotis Ioannidis made it 1-0 after 23 minutes.

The Netherlands offered little going forward and in stoppage time, a bad back pass from Van Bommel led to Giorgos Masouras sliding in a second.

Xavi made a triple change as Gravenberch, Noa Lang and Reijnders came on and they all made a big difference as the Netherlands dominated the second half.

Virgil van Dijk headed one back before Denzel Dumfries had two goals disallowed for offside. Xavi turned to Joshua Zirkzee and debutante Gjivai Zechiel in hope of an equaliser.

The equaliser came in the last minute as Zechiel’s overhead kick dropped into the path of Reijnders who blasted the ball in. Zechiel then came close to a winner but his header was straight at the goalkeeper.

A battling point for the Netherlands, who stay unbeaten under Xavi with a game at home to Serbia to come on Sunday.

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 10 of the Eredivisie ...

The Netherlands came back from 2-0 down to earn a 2-2 draw against Greece in the Nations League. Here is our player ratings for the Netherlands players.

Bart Verbruggen 5.5/10: Wasn’t happy with his defence in the first half. Could have done more for the first goal but did make a good save. Had nothing to do after the break.

Denzel Dumfries 5/10: Struggled in the first half before improving in the second. Had two goals disallowed for offside.

Micky van de Ven 5/10: His pace makes up for the gaps he leaves at times and in the firs half he struggled. Like others he got better in the second but had less to do.

Jurrien Timber 4/10: Looked very much like a player who is not fully fit. Second to everything and gave away fouls in the first half. Was too slow too react for the second goal.

Virgil van Dijk 6/10: Defensively he struggled in the first but his goal got his side back into the game after the break.

Joey Veerman 6.5/10: Always looking to make something happen going forward. Not everything worked but was always available. His corner led to a goal an he almost scored a free kick.

Quinten Timber 4/10: Disastrous display as he gave away foul after foul in the first half. Was rightly hooked at half time.

Guus Til 4/10: Like Timber, he offered nothing in the first half and was rightly taken off. Xavi wanted to give him a chance but he didn’t take it.

Crysencio Summerville 6/10: Was Netherlands only outlet going forward in the first half and is a constant threat on the right hand side.

Ruben van Bommel 4.5/10: Was too static at times and his error led to the second goal. Taken off at the break.

Mexx Meerdink 5/10: Tried but was isolated at times. Had no service in the first half and barely touched the ball in the second.

Noa Lang 7/10: Offered more on the left than Van Bommel as he took the game to the Greek defence. His cross led to the equaliser. Likely to start against Serbia after this display.

Tijjani Reijnders 6.5/10: Came on and scored the equaliser with a good finish. Did a lot more running than Til did and it was much needed energy.

Ryan Gravenberch 6.5/10: Immediately helped the Netherlands midfield gain control back in the second half.

Joshua Zirkzee N/A: Not on the pitch long enough for a review

Gjivai Zechiel N/A: Not on the pitch long enough for a review

FT Greece 2-2 NetherlandsA battling draw after being 2-0 down. Had 2 goals disallowed too! Much better second half but that first half was a disaster

#2444. Zirkzee fires Netherlands to win over Serbia

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 11 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 10 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 9 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 8 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 6 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 5 of the Eredivisie ...

Is AZ Alkmaar's academy now the best in the Netherlands? ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 4 of the Eredivisie ...

It's time for our BIG Dutch Eredivisie season preview! Football-Oranje's ...

A new season of Dutch football is here! Football-Oranje's Michael ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 11 of the Eredivisie ...

Football-Oranje's Michael and Abdul review round 10 of the Eredivisie ...

Netherlands edged out Serbia 2-1 in Eindhoven to move top of their Nations League group.

Xavi decided to name Teun Koopmeiners in the starting eleven but at centre-back alongside Virgil van Dijk, who was earning his 100th cap. Ryan Gravenberch, Tijjani Reijnders and Noa Lang all came into the eleven.

Netherlands made a strong start and missed two good chances to take the lead within the first two minutes. They then dominated possession but it took until the 31st minute for Oranje to take the lead. Koopmeiners free kick evaded everyone and nestled in the corner.

Oranje were only ahead for four minutes as Mihaijo Cvetkovic was allowed far too much space in the Netherlands box to loop in a header. It could have been worse for the Netherlands before the break but Pavlovic squandered a chance when in on goal.

Xavi brought on Joshua Zirkzee for Mexx Meerdink at the break but the best chances early in the second half were for Serbia. Cvetkovic should have put the visitors ahead.

Xavi turned to the bench again with Gjivai Zechiel, Ruben van Bommel and Jorrel Hato all appearing. Reijnders should have made it 2-1 but when through on goal he scooped the ball over the bar.

Netherlands retook the lead after 72 minutes with Denzel Dumfries crossing for Reijnders to square it to an unmarked Zirkzee, who tapped in. It should have been 3-1 straight away but Reijnders was denied through on goal.

The Netherlands sat back and that allowed Serbia to put on pressure. That pressure increased when Micky van de Ven was forced off and Oranje played the last five minutes with only ten men.

Bart Verbruggen made a big save to deny Nemanja Gudelj and Netherlands managed to hold on for the three points.

Netherlands first four games under Xavi see them win two and draw two. They will finish the Nations League at home to Germany and then away to Greece.

FT Netherlands 2-1 SerbiaIt’s three points but far from convincing in the end. Xavi remains unbeaten in his first four games in charge

#2443. Signori e la Nazionale: «Quella ferita non si chiuderà mai», ritorno in azzurro

Il nuovo incarico nell’Under 20 allevia il dolore degli anni difficili; dopo la chiamata, il primo pensiero è andato a Tina.

Beppe Signori torna in azzurro: Malagò gli ha affidato il ruolo di capo delegazione della Nazionale Under 20. L’ex attaccante, che attendeva da tempo una chiamata nel calcio, si prepara al debutto del 12 novembre a Padova contro il Portogallo. Il nuovo incarico riporta con sé l’entusiasmo per una maglia indossata da protagonista e il ricordo degli anni segnati da una vicenda giudiziaria che ha condizionato profondamente la sua vita.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

In vista della partita contro il Portogallo, Signori descrive sensazioni diverse da quelle vissute da calciatore: «È un debutto diverso, ma per certi versi molto più emozionante. Sono carico di entusiasmo, sento una grande responsabilità».

Alla proposta di Malagò, racconta, non ha mai pensato di rispondere negativamente. La considerava un’opportunità attesa a lungo e non avrebbe voluto rinunciarvi per orgoglio o per la rabbia legata a ciò che aveva subito: «Assolutamente mai! Era una grande opportunità, l'aspettavo da tanto».

Signori, intervistato da Il Resto del Carlino, vuole partire da determinazione, sacrificio e passione. Intende aiutare i giovani a comprendere la responsabilità di rappresentare la propria nazione e l’orgoglio di vestire l’azzurro. A suo giudizio, oggi i ragazzi sono abituati ad avere tutto, mentre la sua generazione doveva conquistarsi ogni cosa.

Alla domanda se l’azzurro abbia perso fascino dopo tre Mondiali mancati, risponde senza esitazioni: «No, l'azzurro non passa mai. Quella maglia ti dà una sensazione forte, unica». Ricorda la competizione per un posto ai suoi tempi, quando tra gli attaccanti c’erano anche Baggio, Zola, Mancini, Vialli e Casiraghi. Per questo ritiene che i giovani debbano riconoscere il privilegio di giocare per l’Italia e dimostrare di esserne all’altezza, nonostante le distrazioni esterne e una società orientata, secondo lui, al tutto e subito.

#2441. La réserve renverse Le Cannet-Rocheville sous les yeux de Lopez

Sous les yeux de Christian Lopez, qui a donné le coup d'envoi fictif de cette rencontre entre ses deux anciens clubs, la réserve s'est imposée (2-1) ce samedi sur le fil et sur le terrain du Cannet-Rocheville (Alpes Maritimes) grâce à renversant doublé de Lamine Sonko (photo).

Après un premier quart d'heure plutôt équilibré, l'ASSE a pris l'ascendant mais n'a pas su ouvrir le score malgré deux grosses occasions de son très remuant ailier gauche Etienne Mendy et une tentative de Mamadou Konté sauvée in extremis sur sa ligne par un défenseur.

Sainté a concédé l'ouverture du score à la 56e minute suite à un 6 mètres joué trop vite suivi d'une perte de balle. Les Maralpins ont bien joué le coup sur cette action qui semblait quand même entachée d'une légère faute sur Mamadou Konté.

Boostés par l'apport de leurs entrants, les Verts ont égalisé à la 86e minute sur une ouverture de leur latéral droit Tom Teillol pour Lamine Sonko, qui a résisté au retour de deux défenseurs avant de mettre un petit piqué pour tromper le mal prénommé mais bien nommé Victor Battu.

A la 92e minute, Nathan Kasia a perdu le ballon mais s'est bagarré pour le récupérer et lancer Noah Moulin, dont le centre tendu a été repris victorieusement par le supersub Lamine Sonko. Ce succès permet aux Verts de monter provisoirement à la 2e place du classement, derrière le solide leader Berre.

La réaction de Sylvain Gibert : "On a su faire la différence à la fin du match mais on aurait mérité de mener au score à la pause car la dernière demi-heure de la première période a été très bonne. On a manqué d'efficacité devant le but, c'est donc notre adversaire qui a ouvert le score. Mais on est reparti de l'avant, on a insisté et au final on a été récompensé. C'est la victoire de tout un groupe, les entrants notamment nous ont apporté l'énergie nécessaire pour renverser ce match. Les joueus étaient venus ici pour gagner et ils se sont donnés les moyens d'y parvenir. Il faut les féliciter pour ça. On est content d'avoir décroché ce succès sous les yeux de Christian Lopez, qui nous a fait l'honneur d'être présent et d'échanger avec nous à la fin du match. C'était top car c'est un joueur que j'ai cité la saison dernière dans mes causeries. Je trouve qu'il représente les valeurs du club, cette capacité à ne jamais renoncer, comme l'illustre son mythique intervention sur Oleg Blokhine contre Kiev."

#2440. "He won't be able to leave me out next time!" - Raum sends message to Klopp

RB Leipzig enjoyed a convincing 5-2 victory over FC Augsburg, but David Raum had an additional reason to celebrate. After being left out of Jürgen Klopp's latest Germany squad, the defender used his goal to send a message and vowed to make the national team coach reconsider his decision.

The Leipzig defender admitted that his goal against Augsburg carried some extra motivation after weeks of speculation and media coverage surrounding his national team future.

"There was a little bit of frustration behind the goal. A lot has been written in the media over the past few weeks, which, to put it bluntly, has really annoyed me. So I responded with my goal," Raum explained.

The defender also revealed that he had spoken directly with Klopp following the squad announcement. Despite disagreeing with the decision, Raum emphasised that he respects the national team coach's choice.

"Klopp called me. A lot of newspapers had already written various things about what he supposedly said. I respect his decision, and I told him that. After being involved for a long time, this is the first time I've been left out in a while. I also told him that I'll respond with my performances so that next time he won't be able to leave me out."

Raum added that he wished Klopp and the national team success, stressing that he wants Germany to move forward and become a team the country can be proud of. He also made clear that he intends to contribute again in the future.

Finally, the 28-year-old praised teammate Brajan Gruda, who was included in the latest Germany squad.

"Brajan showed today why he fully deserves his place in the national team. He played brilliantly and has earned it. He should enjoy the experience and perform for Germany just as well as he does here."

#2434. « Cela aurait pu être ma fille » : Thierry Henry exprime sa profonde colère et son indignation sur le plateau de CBS

Sur le plateau de CBS Sports lors de la soirée de Ligue des champions ce mardi 3 novembre 2026, Thierry Henry a tenu à prendre la parole pour évoquer l'attentat raciste survenu dans les Yvelines. Un moment de grande émotion pour l'ancien attaquant des Bleus, profondément touché par ce drame.

Mardi soir, alors que les débats sportifs battaient leur plein autour des affiches européennes, Thierry Henry a choisi d'interrompre l'analyse pour aborder l'attentat raciste perpétré le 23 octobre 2026 à Trappes et La Verrière. Une prise de parole rare et solennelle en plein direct. L'ancien international français a livré un message sincère, visiblement ému par les événements survenus en région parisienne.

Son intervention a immédiatement été saluée sur le plateau de l'émission américaine. Ainsi, ses collègues consultants Jamie Carragher et Micah Richards ont salué ses propos par des applaudissements appuyés, marquant leur respect pour son engagement et sa dignité face à cette tragédie.

Au-delà de la compassion, Thierry Henry a insisté sur son histoire personnelle pour expliquer à quel point ce drame le touchait de près. Père de famille français, noir et originaire de la région parisienne, l'ex-buteur a partagé son sentiment de révolte et d'inquiétude pour les siens. « Je suis noir, je suis français, je suis père de famille. Cela aurait pu être mon frère, cela aurait pu être ma fille. Alors, cela doit cesser », a-t-il déclaré avec gravité face aux caméras.

L'ancien champion a également exprimé son incompréhension face aux remises en question de l'appartenance nationale. « À un moment donné, ça suffit. On se promène dans la rue et on se fait tuer, tout simplement. Est-ce que nous devons toujours prouver que nous sommes français ? Nous sommes français et cela ne changera jamais, quoi qu’il arrive », a poursuivi Thierry Henry sur le plateau de CBS Sports.

En effet, l'attaque survenue le 23 octobre 2026 à Trappes et La Verrière a fait un mort et sept blessés, dont une jeune fille de 12 ans. L'assaillant présumé, un homme de 39 ans, a été mis en examen et placé en détention provisoire après avoir reconnu avoir ciblé délibérément des personnes musulmanes, africaines ou nord-africaines. Un choc immense pour les familles.

Thierry Henry a tenu à honorer la mémoire de Pengo Bossio Wangi, combattant de MMA de 26 ans tué lors de cet attentat : « Je souhaite simplement rendre hommage et témoigner mon respect aux familles des victimes, et évidemment à la famille de Pengo, qui a perdu la vie simplement parce qu’il était noir. Je voulais simplement apporter un peu de soutien à toutes les personnes qui ont souffert de cet événement. Comme je l’ai dit, je vais me répéter : ça suffit ».

#2433. « Une décision inattendue, que l’OL regrette profondément » : Le communiqué amer de l'Olympique Lyonnais officialisant le départ de Paulo Fonseca

Ce mercredi 4 novembre 2026, l'Olympique Lyonnais a officialisé le départ de Paulo Fonseca de ses fonctions d'entraîneur de l'équipe professionnelle vers le Sporting Portugal. Dans un communiqué au ton particulièrement piquant, la direction rhodanienne a fustigé le timing de cette décision inattendue et l'engagement du technicien.

La nouvelle a surpris tout le monde à Lyon. Alors que son contrat avec l'OL courait initialement jusqu'au 30 juin 2027, Paulo Fonseca a lui-même informé ses dirigeants de sa volonté de partir. Le technicien de 53 ans a en effet été approché ces derniers jours par le Sporting Portugal, un club auquel il est particulièrement attaché. Pour racheter son contrat, la formation portugaise versera d'ailleurs une indemnité de 5 millions d'euros à l'OL. Toutefois, cette indemnité ne console pas les décideurs lyonnais.

Arrivé le 31 janvier 2025, Paulo Fonseca a dirigé son 83e et dernier match dimanche 1er novembre 2026 lors de la victoire contre Angers (2-0) au Groupama Stadium. Il quitte Lyon en laissant le club à la 3e place de Ligue 1 avec 17 points, pour un bilan de 5 victoires, 2 nuls et 2 défaites. Une dynamique sportive positive qui rend la séparation d'autant plus amère pour l'OL.

Au-delà de la surprise créée par cette demande, ce sont surtout les termes choisis par l'OL dans son communiqué qui détonnent. Loin des formules polies d'usage, l'état-major lyonnais a pointé du doigt l'attitude de son entraîneur. La direction a ainsi estimé qu'il « n'était pas dans l'intérêt du club de poursuivre une collaboration qui ne pouvait plus garantir le niveau d'engagement total indispensable à la réussite du projet ». Un reproche cinglant adressé au Portugais.

En effet, le club rhodanien a insisté sur le fait que dans « une période aussi déterminante de la saison », l'ensemble des forces devait être « entièrement mobilisé et concentré sur les objectifs ». Face à un entraîneur désireux d'ailleurs, l'OL a préféré trancher immédiatement dans le vif. La direction s'active désormais pour identifier un successeur au profil capable de s'inscrire dans la continuité du projet sportif et des principes développés ces derniers mois.

Dans l'immédiat, l'Olympique Lyonnais a dû réagir rapidement pour gérer le quotidien. C'est Jorge Maciel qui a été désigné pour assurer l'intérim à la tête de l'équipe professionnelle jusqu'à la prochaine trêve internationale de novembre 2026. Le technicien va devoir prendre le relais sans temps d'adaptation avant deux déplacements importants.

Jorge Maciel dirigera ainsi les Gones face à la Real Sociedad ce jeudi 5 novembre 2026 à 18h45 au stade d'Anoeta, dans le cadre de la Ligue Europa. Dans la foulée, le technicien conduira également le groupe lyonnais lors du déplacement en Ligue 1 à Brest, prévu le dimanche 8 novembre 2026 à 17h15.
